# Skill upgrade list — every live skill against the 8/10 standard

Audit date 2026-10-23, tree `claude/sweet-newton-c8wrv1` @ `07523be2`. Independent auditor (Opus medium). Evidence only: nothing was rendered for this list. The one new reading is a node read of the generators (12 seeded items per skill through `generateQuestionFor`, the same harness `ws-node-lint` uses) to record which kit template each skill emits and whether `getProvider()` is real; no page or screen was drawn.

**The bar (owner, clarified 2026-10-23).** A skill **meets the standard** when it was redone to the black-and-white worksheet contract (`WORKSHEET_DESIGN_STANDARD.md`, `PEDAGOGY_STANDARD.md`, the sheet kit: black and white, Andika, big digits, one boxed cell per problem, answer key, screen parity) **and** an independent critic passed it at 8 or better on every criterion, as recorded in `design/STATUS.md` and the critic reports. It does **not** also need to have passed under the newer process rules of 2026-10-23 (S and L graded in full, rules 0a / 0b). Where a skill that meets the standard has machine findings pinned today, the list says **re-check suggested**; it is not downgraded.

## Summary for the owner

**715 live skills** (723 entries in `SKILLS`, 8 retired skipped).

| Status | Skills | What it means |
|---|---|---|
| MEETS-STANDARD | 4 | redone to the black-and-white kit and passed an independent critic at 8+ |
| IN-PROGRESS | 6 | the SU-C pilot (six no-regroup subtraction skills on `stack`), critic r1 failed, fix round 1 done |
| UNKNOWN | 11 | preset skills of a host that meets the standard; their own option values were never graded |
| NEEDS-UPGRADE | 694 | not redone to the kit yet, or redone but never passed a critic at 8+ |

In plain words:
- **Only 4 skills meet the standard today**: `multiplication:count_by_tables`, `composing:hundreds_chart_fill`, `composing:number_chart_fill` (Wave 1 Lane C, critic r5 PASS) and `division:div_facts` (div_facts forms, critic r4 PASS). Nothing else has a recorded critic pass at 8 on its Practice pages, keys and screen hosts.
- **Of the 694 that need upgrading:** 387 still print through the old (legacy) cell, 48 are part-way onto a kit template, and 259 are already drawn by a kit template but were never passed. 103 of them are preset skills (they follow their host), 63 are mixed pools (they follow their members and the Mixed practice composer) and 59 are twins the family audit says to merge into another skill.
- **Teaching content is thin on most skills:** only 261 of 715 have a real provider (worked steps, wrong answers, instruction strings); the rest print the default adapter, so a worked example does not print for them.
- **No template is certified yet** (`design/audit/certified/` does not exist), so by process rule 0a every build is on Opus medium until the first (template, kind of worksheet) pair passes. `stack` × Single skill is the nearest, through the SU-C pilot.
- **14 skills were graded at 8 or better in some round but do not count**: the round was at size L only (the 2026-09-25 regrades), or the graded tree was a lane branch that was paused on 2026-09-26 and never merged (figures-r8, geometry-r2, pv-r3, k2-r2). They are the cheapest to bring over the bar; they are listed below.

### Recommended order (IMPROVEMENT_PLAN §1.5 template-first, §4.1 order of work)

1. **Finish the SU-C pilot** (6 skills, `stack` × Single skill): fix round 2 at Opus medium (rule 0a), precritic green and the sibling comparison (rule 0b), critic r2 at S and L in full. Its pass is the first certificate and the benchmark for every other `stack` skill.
2. **Wave 0 and Mixed pages M1–M5** as planned (§4.1 rows 1–2). Many pinned SPACE / SIZE findings sit in shared code (forced 2-column `stack` cells H13, 7 pt anchor step numbers TY-11, the sections / mixed composer), so they are fixed once in the engine, not per skill.
3. **The worksheet lane (§4.1 row 2b): certify Single skill and Sections, then the provider pass family by family, largest first.** The skills already drawn by a kit template come first, because they are copies on an existing drawing: `stack` (addition and subtraction columns), `word-work` (the story skills), `pv` (place value and rounding), `frac-model`, `equation`, `clock`, `timeline`, `coins`, `count-row`, `counters`, `number-line`. Re-grade the near-miss skills below in the same pass.
4. **Legacy skills that map onto an existing template** (migrate, then copy): `equation` (algebra, order of operations, number theory), `words` (the 50 vocabulary skills, all legacy), `frac-model` (fraction operations), `stack` (decimals), `fact`, `division`.
5. **New templates by leverage** (§1.5 step 1): `shape-grid` → `bar-model` → `data-table` → `picture-row` → `measure-scale` → `graph-axes` → `angle-kit` → `solid-kit` → `coord-grid`, plus `long-multiplication`; PR #13 (shape drawings) is the starting point of `shape-grid` and `solid-kit`. Each: signed card → one model skill at Opus medium → full critic → golden renders → the rest as data at Opus low → batch critic.
6. **Mixed pools** (63) are graded through the certified Mixed practice composer once their members pass; **merges** (59) are done as aliases (never a splice, CLAUDE.md) and need no build of their own.
7. **Re-checks** for the four skills that meet the standard, where noted below, at the next deploy's inheritance audit.

## How to read the per-skill tables

- **Template**: the kit cell the generator emits (`q.cell.template`) over 12 seeded items; `legacy` = no kit cell (old print path); `(+ legacy n/12)` = part-migrated.
- **Provider**: `real (k/3 core)` = `getProvider().real` lists strings / workedSteps / wrongAnswer; `default adapter` = none real.
- **Status**: as defined above. Lane-only critic grades (rounds on lane trees that were never merged) and single-role rounds (anchors, Guided, Error analysis) are evidence, never a pass.
- **Machine findings** are the pinned baselines of 2026-10-23, by defect class (`design/status/defect-classes.md`):
  - `print-lint L/S/M` — `design/audit/baselines/print-lint.json` (L), `print-lint.S.json`, `print-lint.M.json`, counts at L / S / M. Classes: SIZE = L-SIZE (TY-11); SPACE = L-DENSITY (H13, PAGEFILL, PG-23, LONE); FONT = L-FONT TY-1/TY-2; NOTATION = L-FONT TY-7 (slashed fraction) and L-EMOJI TY-6; INK = L-INK INK-*/LS-4 and L-EMOJI INK-7; CLIP = L-OVERFLOW and L-INK ILLEGIBLE; KEY = L-KEY (AK-2, AK-4); SLOT = L-ANSAREA; VERBS = L-VERBS (screen verb on paper); TAGLINE = L-CCSS SC-5.
  - `node-lint` — `design/audit/baselines/node-lint.json`: NOTATION, SIZE (L1), GIVEAWAY (L3), INSTRUCTION (L6), PATTERN (L10), REPEAT; counts of pinned finding keys.
  - `options-verify` — failing option values in `design/audit/baselines/options-verify.json` (number-line support `nl*` values counted apart).
  - `MC on screen` — `design/audit/baselines/content-audit-mc.json` (a written answer offered as buttons on screen, H8).
  - `matrix` — red rows of `design/audit/matrix/baseline.json` (only the SU-C six and their stack siblings are in it so far).
  - `screen-slots.json` and `viewport-check.json` pin no findings for any skill.
- **Catalogue**: the family audit verdict (keep / fix / split / merge / redo) and its defect list, from `design/catalogue/<family>.md` (line cited) and `.overrides.json`; skills added after the audit (presets and later builds) have none.
- **Effort**: `copy` = on an existing kit template (fix + provider, or migrate from legacy then copy); `needs a new template` = no kit drawing exists for it yet; `redo` = the catalogue says redo; preset / pool / merge as above.
- Paths are shortened: `runs/` = `design/audit/runs/`, `catalogue/` = `design/catalogue/`.
